Woit compares cohomology with trivial-coefficient cohomology using exact invariants in the finite-dimensional semisimple/compact context. Exactness is false on arbitrary U(g)-modules (for g=sl2 the augmentation U(g)->C has no invariant lift: left multiplication by a nonzero Lie algebra element is injective in U(g), so the regular module has no nonzero invariants), and Whitehead H1/H2 vanishing alone is not the stated all-degree tensor formula. The nilradical items do not need this result, but its source/claimed-published-coverage qualification needs owner reconciliation. ## Concrete owner findings

- **PDE-14 promised additions:** the controlling `research/plan-pde-track.md` lines 3611–3627 explicitly name `thm-hardy-inequality-for-w-one-p-zero-on-a-half-space`, `cex-boundary-hardy-inequality-needs-zero-trace-or-compact-support`, and `thm-gagliardo-nirenberg-derivative-interpolation-in-the-stated-integer-regime`. They are absent from the current batch-4 manifest/page. “No consumer” and “a different algebra proof route” do not independently cancel those promised standalone results. Their three owning rows are `owner-decision`; no unreviewed proof was authored to conceal this scope discrepancy. The deferred critical bubble and dual estimate do have exact current replacements: `ex-normalised-critical-bubbles-converge-weakly-but-not-strongly-at-p-star` states both scale-invariant norms, and `lem-ltwo-and-divergence-data-embed-in-h-minus-one` gives the L2-to-H-minus-one bound when the divergence data are zero.
- **Gradient convexity:** PDE-21 design item 12 describes gradient convexity, while current `thm-direct-method-for-convex-integral-functionals` requires joint convexity in the value and gradient. Maiale Theorem 2.51 supplies the more general gradient-convex lower-semicontinuity statement. The current proof was read and is sound for its joint-convex hypotheses; its richer advertised scope is left for owner reconciliation. It was not narrowed further or independently stamped.
- **Körner Lemma 12.8:** the [cited complete topological-groups notes](https://web.archive.org/web/2024id_/https://www.dpmms.cam.ac.uk/~twk10/Topg.pdf) state a convolution of two L2 functions on the dual is the transform of an L1 function. They do not state “every L2 function is a product of transforms.” Both batch-26 rows describing that latter claim are owner-held. Current FR-17 has a separate range-density argument, whose validity is not decided by this incorrect source-row name.
- **Grigoryan Theorem 1.31:** the [complete cited notes](https://web.math.ucsb.edu/~grigoryan/246B/lecs/246B.pdf) identify it as zero-trace Poincaré on a domain bounded in one direction, not a boundary Rellich lemma. The batch-9 excluded-source row needs its locator corrected; the current trace-compactness route remains the local fractional one.
- **Vivaldi Lemma C.15:** the [cited Appendix C](https://maths.qmul.ac.uk/~fvivaldi/teaching/ETAD/NotesI.pdf) states an arbitrary-LCA-group result for an absolutely continuous measure. Two batch-26 reasons incorrectly call it Euclidean. Current `thm-riemann-lebesgue-lemma-on-lca-groups` supplies the L1-density version; the coverage-domain description still needs correction.
- **HAL parabolic source:** the [actual cited paper](https://web.archive.org/web/20240609034644id_/https://hal.science/hal-04312924v1/file/10.1515_anona-2020-0015.pdf) is Guibé–Mokrane–Tahraoui–Vallet's deterministic pseudomonotone parabolic Lewy–Stampacchia paper. Its extracted text has no stochastic/Ito occurrence. The batch-16 reason about stochastic predictable-process material is unsupported by that body. This does not reject the separately repaired static obstacle item.
- **Schnaubelt Theorem 1.29:** the [cited evolution notes](https://iana.math.kit.edu/downloads/iana3/schnaubelt/Skripten/evgl-skript.pdf) characterize C0 groups via the generators A and -A. The batch-17 reason instead calls it an abstract-Cauchy-problem characterization. The semigroup/group boundary can justify an exclusion, but the reason needs the correct source statement.
- **Function classes and source qualifications:** Hunter Theorem 3.35 has local integrability, Lp gradient and vanishing-at-infinity hypotheses; the current W1p result adds Lp membership of the function. Calling that current result stronger is unsupported. Woit's semisimple invariant/cohomology comparison needs its coefficient category kept explicit: invariants are not exact on arbitrary U(g)-modules, and Whitehead's degree-one/two results alone are not an all-degree tensor formula. Teschl's general time-dependent parabolic section is not supplied merely by the current elliptic Schauder and autonomous analytic-semigroup pages. These reasons remain owner-held; no defective published item has been inferred from a source alone.
- **Uninstantiated continuations:** general parabolic/Dini, nonlinear Monge–Ampère, arbitrary-domain Perron/capacity/Wiener, and relaxation/Lavrentiev continuations retain owner destinations. No new page or reading-order change is authorized by this report.
